rolling without slipping  ^OMNXu8Yy

rotational work ^5oZ5wAmH

angular momentum ^dsr2eGVi

Consider the cue ball problem explained in Lecture-25 Problems Notes,
Slides 21-25. If you hit the ball at h=4/5R, the ball will spin and slide
simultaneously immediately after the hit and eventually roll without
slipping. a) What will the speed of its center of mass be then? b) At what
time, t, will this happen? c) compute the work of the friction, Wf, and the
change of kinetic energy in the system. ^wqPLwYcj

PAY ATTENTION
TO SIGNS!! ^VQfIPM9p
